{"antonyms":[{"word":"come down"},{"word":"go down"}],"forms":[{"form":"comes up","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"coming up","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"came up","tags":["past"]},{"form":"come up","tags":["participle","past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Unless anything comes up, I'll be there every day this week.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To emerge or become known, especially unexpectedly."],"tags":["idiomatic","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The movie didn't come up to our expectations.","type":"example"},{"text":"They've looked for him everywhere, and come up dry.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be revealed to have a certain value, quality, or status."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"At some point in the conversation my name came up, and I readily agreed to their proposition.","type":"example"},{"text":"Be ready for when your turn comes up.","type":"example"},{"text":"The proposal came up before the committee.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To come to attention and present oneself; to arrive or appear."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He came up before a judge and was fined a thousand dollars.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To appear (before a judge or court)."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The summer holidays are coming up.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To draw near in time."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We're coming up on the interview in the last half-hour of the program.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To approach a time or scheduled event."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You have to come up to here to ride this roller-coaster.","type":"example"},{"text":"He only comes up around this high.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To reach in height."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It'll be warmer once the sun comes up.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To rise (above the horizon)."],"raw_tags":["of a heavenly body"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I could tell from her expression that she was coming up already.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To begin to feel the effects of a recreational drug."],"tags":["UK","intransitive","slang"]},{"glosses":["To arrive at the university. (Compare go down, send down.)"],"qualifier":"Oxford University","tags":["UK","intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"His shift came up, so he had to go to work.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To happen or occur."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["intransitive","slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I came up in Baltimore.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To grow up; to experience a childhood."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["intransitive","slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Watch out for him, he's been coming up lately.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To do well or be successful."],"qualifier":"African-American Vernacular","tags":["intransitive","slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I came up the ladder carefully, holding the bucket in my right hand.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see come, up."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I was standing on the corner when Nick came up and asked for a cigarette.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To come towards; to approach."],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":546196,"upstream_offset":2496272051,"upstream_record_sha256":"d342aacad7b77c80139897928a02365db0f1ceb930a19a118199c5396ec6d39f","word":"come up"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"poises","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A state of balance, equilibrium or stability."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Composure; freedom from embarrassment or affectation."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Mien; bearing or deportment of the head or body."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A condition of hovering, or being suspended."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A CGS unit of dynamic viscosity equal to one dyne-second per square centimetre."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["Weight; an amount of weight, the amount something weighs."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The weight, or mass of metal, used in weighing, to balance the substance weighed."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["That which causes a balance; a counterweight."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"poiz"},{"ipa":"/pɔɪz/"}],"upstream_line":266739,"upstream_offset":1333304137,"upstream_record_sha256":"34f019cbeae3888b8410b1f3dd6387a812aa5d6bc6e38e50461fafa571f508d4","word":"poise"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"poises","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"poising","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"poised","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"poised","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To hang in equilibrium; to be balanced or suspended; hence, to be in suspense or doubt."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To counterpoise; to counterbalance."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To be of a given weight; to weigh."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To add weight to, to weigh down."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["To hold (something) with or against something else in equilibrium; to balance, counterpose."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I poised the crowbar in my hand, and waited.","type":"example"},{"text":"to poise the scales of a balance","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To hold (something) in equilibrium, to hold balanced and ready; to carry (something) ready to be used."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The rock was poised precariously on the edge of the cliff.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To keep (something) in equilibrium; to hold suspended or balanced."]},{"glosses":["To ascertain, as if by balancing; to weigh."]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"poiz"},{"ipa":"/pɔɪz/"}],"upstream_line":266740,"upstream_offset":1333324399,"upstream_record_sha256":"96b4641937200e97feb733526c61c83cecf53ef38a80ebde8d9122a762034909","word":"poise"}
